Block LP function:
```
def build_block_F(f, B):
    n=len(f); L=n//B; prefix=np.cumsum([0]+f)
    k=np.arange(2*n-1)
    F=np.empty((2*n-1,B))
    for j in range(B):
        start=j*L; end=(j+1)*L-1
        low=np.maximum(k-end,0)
        high=np.minimum(k-start+1,n)
        F[:,j]=prefix[high]-prefix[low]
    return F, L
```
Need ensure high not negative? k-start+1 can be negative for k<start-1, but min with n and maybe max? If high <0, prefix[high] invalid. Use low=np.maximum(np.minimum(k-end, n), 0)? Let's derive: valid t in [k-end, k-start]. We want sum prefix[t] for t from low to high where low = max(0, k-end), high+1? Actually prefix index p sum f[0:p]. We need sum f[t] for t in [A,B] inclusive. low = max(0, k-end). high_exclusive = min(n, k-start+1). If high_exclusive <=0, sum=0. So high_excl = min(n, k-start+1); if high_excl<0 set 0. low = max(0, k-end); low=min(low,n). Then F=prefix[high_excl]-prefix[low] with max(high_excl,low). Use np.where.
Simpler loop j:
```
A = k - end
Bexc = k - start + 1
low = np.clip(A, 0, n)
high = np.clip(Bexc, 0, n)
val = prefix[high] - prefix[low]
val = np.maximum(val, 0) # if high<low? due clipping; but A<=Bexc always since L>=1. Clipping can make low > high if both outside? e.g. k<start: Bexc<0 high=0, A<0 low=0 =>0. k>2n? not. k-end >n low=n, Bexc>n high=n =>0. If low>high? maybe if A>n and Bexc<0 impossible. But we can val=np.where(high>=low, prefix[high]-prefix[low],0)
```

def resample_to_length(p, n):

def fast_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); s=sum; if s<1e-300 return inf
    b=fft_convolution(a); m=max(b); if not finite return inf
    return 2*n*m/(s*s)

Now, L-BFGS objective function details:
```
def softmax_full(v, n):
    z = np.empty(n)
    z[0]=0.0
    z[1:]=v
    # stable: z -= max(z)
    zmax = np.max(z)
    if not finite: ...
    z -= zmax
    e = np.exp(np.clip(z, -745, 709))
    s = e.sum()
    if s<=0: e=zeros; e[0]=1; s=1
    return e/s
```
But if v has large range >700, exp underflow; okay. However if zmax large positive for some index, subtract max. The gauge fixed z0=0, but if zmax=1000 at index k, z0=-1000 -> exp underflow, a concentrates at k. That's okay maybe but gradient? We'll clip z to [-700,700] after shift? If shift makes min -1000, exp min underflow 0. a has zero; gradient wrt that coordinate has a_i=0 so grad_z=0, L-BFGS won't recover. That's bad if we clip too aggressively. Use stable softmax without hard clipping below -745 (underflow to 0) but if a=0, log z can increase? Gradient for a_i=0 is 0, so ever stuck at zero. Need avoid exact zeros. Use adding epsilon to a before log? In L-BFGS parameter z, if exp underflows to 0, coordinate dead. To prevent, clip z to [-30,30] after subtract max? That enforces min a relative max ~e^-30 ~1e-13. Is that okay? Previous sequence may have smaller values but maybe not critical. We can set bound [-35,35]. But if true optimal has some near-zero, 1e-13 okay for n=8192; score effect negligible. Use clip z to [-35,35] after gauge? But L-BFGS variable can exceed; in objective, we clip z before softmax, making objective flat for large v beyond clip, gradient zero. L-BFGS may wander. Better use bounds [-35,35] in L-BFGS-B so it won't ask outside. Then softmax stable. Use bnds for v. If some optimal ratios >e^35, not likely. Pseudo:
```
def exact_polish(a0, deadline, rng):
   a=normalize(a0)
   best=score(a)
   step=1.0
   no=0
   it=0
   while time<deadline:
      b=conv(a); M=max(b)
      cur=2*n*M
      if cur<best: update reset no
      else no++
      if no>2000 break
      beta_dyn = max(1000, 1000*(1+it/100))? Use fixed 100000 maybe.
      G,w=smooth_max(b, 100000)
      s=corr_pw(a,w); g=2*s; grad=g-mean(g)
      norm...
      accepted=False
      for alpha in geometric from step:
          q=project_simplex(a-alpha*grad,1)
          sc=fast_score(q)
          if sc < cur - 1e-15:
              a=q; accepted=True; break
      if accepted: adjust step
      else: step*=0.5; if too small perturb restart
```

active bool, list

   best_x=None; best_violation=inf

```
def construct_b2_candidate(n, target_K, max_col, rng, time_limit):
    best_score=inf; best_active=None; best_max=target max? 
    for restart in range(5):
       cnt=np.zeros(2*n-1,dtype=np.int16)
       active=np.zeros(n,dtype=bool)
       S=[]
       for step in range(target_K):
          chosen=None; best_viol=inf
          # try candidates: first low numbers? combine random and from step
          for _ in range(200):
             x = int(rng.integers(n))
             if active[x]: continue
             viol=0
             if cnt[2*x] > max_col-1: viol += 100
             # loop S
             for y in S:
                c=cnt[x+y]
                if c > max_col-2: viol += 1 + c
             if viol==0:
                chosen=x; break
             if viol < best_viol:
                best_viol=viol; best_x=x
          if chosen is None:
             if best_viol == 1: maybe choose? For start with max_col=3, violation 1 means old count=2? Need need old≤1. Could choose if allow max 4. We'll not.
             break
          # add
          active[chosen]=True; S.append(chosen)
          cnt[2*chosen] +=1
          for y in S[:-1]: cnt[chosen+y] += 2
       # after build, maybe hill_climb to reduce max and add
       ...
```

    old_bucketmax = int(bucket[old_max]) if old_max < len(bucket) else 0

    for _ in range(10):

    else: return maxc, False

def _delta_count(cnt,bucket,maxc,s,delta):

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
   start=time.monotonic(); reserve=180; deadline=start+budget-reserve
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   # Determine lengths to try
   try budget int/float
   if budget_s < 300: lengths=[2048]
   elif budget_s < 800: lengths=[4096]
   elif budget_s < 1600: lengths=[8192]
   else: lengths=[16384]  # maybe [8192,16384]? primary 16384
   # If global height_sequence_1 length maybe use its length as candidate? but we resample.
   best_seq=None; best_score=inf
   for n in lengths:
       phase_deadline = deadline
       seq, score = optimize_length(n, deadline, rng, budget_s)
       if score < best_score: best_seq=seq; best_score=score
       if len(lengths)>1 or time left small break
   return list(best_seq)
```

def support_refine_from_indices(n, indices, rng, deadline, max_col=3, K_target=None):
   S=sorted(set(indices[:min(len,K_target)]))
   if K_target and len(S)<K_target: fill random inactive to K_target.
   build cnt, bucket, maxc
   best_score = 2*n*maxc/(len(S)**2)
   best_state copy
   while time<deadline:
      if len(S) < K_target and maxc <= max_col:
          try add feasible several times
      else:
          try swaps first-improve
      if score improved update best
      if no improvement for many trials break (or random restart perturb)
   return active array as sequence with floor, maxc
```

   * Phase A: If maxc ≤3, try add feasible random candidates until no add in T trials.

   * Periodically, if K < K_target and maxc≤3 but no add, try "forced" swaps with objective `bucket_at_3` maybe to free sums.

Let's implement `support_local_search`:
```
def support_local_search(n, S0, K_target, rng, deadline, max_steps...):
    S = list(set(S0))
    if len(S) > K_target: keep top? For support, we can start with K_target; if less, fill random.
    active = np.zeros(n,bool); for x in S active[x]=True
    bucket_size=K_target+5
    cnt,bucket,maxc = build_counts(S,n,bucket_size)
    # track state copy for best
    best_S = S.copy(); best_score = 2*n*maxc/(len(S)**2)
    cur_score=best_score
    no_change=0
    trials=0
    while time<deadline and no_change<limit:
       trials++
       K=len(S)
       # attempt add if K<K_target and random choose
       if K < K_target and rng.random()<0.5:
           x=random inactive up to 10 times
           if found:
              old_max=maxc; old_bucket=bucket[maxc]; old_score=cur_score
              # add x with deltas
              maxc=delta(2x,+1); for y in S: delta(x+y,+2)
              active[x]=True; S.append(x)
              new_score=2*n*maxc/(K+1)^2
              new_bucket=bucket[maxc]
              if new_score < old_score - 1e-12 or (new_max<=3 and new_score<old_score*1.02) or rng.random()<0.05 and new_score<old_score*1.1:
                  accept, no_change=0, best if improved
              else:
                  revert remove x (deltas -), no_change++
       else:
           # swap i,j
           if support_swap_trial accepts (based on score/bucket) ...
           else trial incomplete
       if score improved update best
       if trials%10000 print
    return best sequence
```
For reversion of add: Remove x: for y in S (including x?) before removal, for y: if y==x delta(2x,-1) else delta(x+y,-2); active false; S.remove(x). The order of reverting addition: we added x after S old; to remove, current S includes x. For y in current S: if y==x delta 2x -1; else delta x+y -2. Then remove.

For swap trial, similar but accept/revert. We can implement a single top-level `support_swap_or_add`? To keep code manageable, write `support_try_add` and `support_try_swap` top-level.

Let's implement each robustly.

### normalize_simplex
```
def normalize_simplex(p):
   p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64).copy()
   if p.size==0: return p
   p=np.where(np.isfinite(p),p,0.0)
   p=np.maximum(p,0.0)
   s=float(p.sum())
   if s<=1e-300:
      return np.full(p.shape,1.0/p.size)
   p *= 1.0/s
   return p
```

### fft_convolution
As before. Ensure nonnegative.

### smooth_max_and_weights
Returns G,w. If beta<=0 uniform. Use stable.

### corr_pw
As before.

### fast_score
```
def fast_score(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   n=a.size
   s=float(a.sum())
   if s<1e-300: return inf
   b=fft_convolution(a)
   m=float(b.max())
   if not finite: return inf
   return 2.0*n*m/(s*s)
```
For very large n, b length ~2n; okay.

### resample_to_length
```
def resample_to_length(p,n):
   p=np.asarray(p,float)
   p=np.where(finite,p,0); p=max(0)
   if p.size==0 or n==1...
   old=(arange(p.size)+0.5)/p.size
   new=(arange(n)+0.5)/n
   q=np.interp(new,old,p)
   return normalize(q)
```
If p negative, clipped.

### structured_starts

- uniform
- linear ramp: exp(a*x) with a in [-3,3]
- two-level half (1±eps)
- endpoint-heavy: maybe sequence with one endpoint high? We can generate `a=exp(-λ*(x + (1-x)))`? To make asymmetric: `a_i = exp(α*(i/n - 0.5))` normalized. Already ramp.
- random Fourier: `z=rng.normal(n)+1j*rng.normal(n); a=exp(real(ifft(z))/sum)`
- Tent? `a=1+eps*cos(2π i/n)`
Ensure positive. Limit count.

### log_lbfgs objective
Top-level:
```
def log_lbfgs_objective(v, n, beta, ref, idx):
    z = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    z[ref]=0.0
    z[idx]=v
    # stable softmax
    zmax = float(np.max(z))
    if not finite: ...
    z = z - zmax
    e = np.exp(np.clip(z, -745, 700))
    s = e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: e = zeros; e[ref]=1; s=1
    a=e/s
    b=fft_convolution(a)
    G,w=smooth_max_and_weights(b,beta)
    if not finite(G): return inf, zeros
    svec=corr_pw(a,w)
    g=2.0*svec
    mean_g = dot(a,g)
    grad_z = a*(g - mean_g)
    val = n*G
    grad = grad_z[idx]*n
    grad = np.where(finite(grad), grad,0)
    val = float(val) if finite else inf
    return val, grad
```
But if `z` has max huge, clip e underflow; with bounds -40/40 relative to ref, max z could be 40, min -40. zmax maybe 40, exp ok. If ref not max, some z=40, zmax 40, ref z=-40, exp(-80) underflow? Actually z - zmax: ref -40, min -80; exp(-80) 1.8e-35 >0, within bounds? If bounds [-40,40] on v and ref=0, z values [-40,40], zmax=40 -> min -80, exp underflow? e^-80 ~1.8e-35, fine not zero (double min 1e-308). Good.

```
def exact_gradient_polish(a0, deadline, rng):
   a=normalize(a0); best=fast_score(a); step=0.1; no=0
   while time<deadline-5:
      b=conv(a); M=max; cur=2nM
      if cur<best: best=cur; best_a=a; no=0 else no++
      if no>1000: break
      beta=max(1e6, 5000/M)?? Use smooth weights with beta=1e-3/M? Actually beta factor 5000.
      G,w=smooth_max(b,beta)
      svec=corr_pw(a,w); g=2svec; grad=g-mean(g)
      gnorm=norm(grad)
      if gnorm<1e-14: break
      accepted=False
      # step scale: grad ~1/n? For n=16384 grad~6e-5, p~6e-5. step maybe 1.
      for alpha in [step, step*0.5,... 1e-3*step]:
          q=project_simplex(a - alpha*grad,1)
          sc=fast_score(q)
          if sc < cur - 1e-12:
             a=q; accepted=True; break
      if accepted: adjust step
      else: step*=0.5; if step<1e-6: perturb; no++
```
Previous full_refine used step 1 and many alphas. We can implement but time limited. It may not improve over L-BFGS. Keep short.

### support functions detailed

`build_support_counts(S,n,bucket_size)`:
```
cnt=np.zeros(2*n-1,dtype=np.int16)
# S list Python ints
m=len(S)
for ii in range(m):
   i=S[ii]; cnt[i+i]+=1
   for jj in range(ii+1,m):
       cnt[i+S[jj]] += 2
bucket=np.zeros(bucket_size,dtype=np.int32)
# Use bincount:
nz = cnt[cnt > 0]
if nz.size:
   # counts up to max
   maxc=int(nz.max())
   bucket=np.zeros(max(bucket_size, maxc+1), dtype=np.int32)
   # bincount with minlength maxc+1
   bucket_c = np.bincount(nz, minlength=maxc+1)
   bucket[:min(len(bucket_c), len(bucket))] = bucket_c[:...]
else maxc=0
# If bucket size smaller than maxc+1, resize
if bucket.size <= maxc:
   bucket.resize(maxc+1)
   ...
return cnt,bucket,maxc
```
But if maxc>=bucket_size, we need fill full. Simpler: after cnt, maxc=cnt.max(); bucket=np.zeros(max(bucket_size,maxc+1), int32); `bucket[:maxc+1]=np.bincount(cnt, minlength=maxc+1)`? ```
def support_weight_objective(v, S, n, beta, ref, idx):
    K=len(S)
    z=np.empty(K); z[ref]=0; z[idx]=v
    # softmax
    zmax=max(z); z-=zmax; e=exp(clip); p=e/sum
    full=np.zeros(n,float); full[S]=p
    b=fft_convolution(full); G,w=smooth_max(b,beta)
    if inf return inf,zeros
    svec=corr_pw(full,w)
    g=svec[S]*2.0
    mean=float(np.dot(p,g))
    grad_z=p*(g-mean)
    val=n*G
    return val, grad_z[idx]*n
```

`_delta_count` with lists:
```
def _delta_count(cnt,bucket,maxc,s,delta):
   old=cnt[s]; new=old+delta
   if new<0: new=0
   if new >= len(bucket):
      ext = new+1-len(bucket)
      bucket.extend([0]*ext)
   bucket[old]-=1
   cnt[s]=new
   bucket[new]+=1
   if new > maxc:
      maxc=new
   elif old == maxc and bucket[old] == 0:
      while maxc>0 and bucket[maxc]==0:
         maxc-=1
   return maxc
```
If old>=len(bucket) not possible. This is faster.

Support local search K maybe up to 300, counts max <=K. Good.

#### Code details:

`structured_starts`:
```
def structured_starts(n, rng, max_count=8):
    starts=[]
    starts.append(np.full(n,1.0/n))
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    for a in (-4,-2,2,4):
       p=np.exp(a*(x-0.5)); p=normalize_simplex(p); starts.append(p)
    for eps in (0.5,0.8):
       p=np.ones(n); p[:n//2]=1+eps; p[n//2:]=1-eps; p=np.maximum(p,1e-6); p=normalize_simplex(p); starts.append(p)
    for k in (1,2,3):
       p=1+0.5*np.cos(2*np.pi*k*x); normalize; starts.append
    # random Fourier
    size=1; while size<n: size<<=1
    for _ in range(2):
       zc=rng.normal(size=size)+1j*rng.normal(size=size)
       z=np.real(np.fft.ifft(zc))[:n]
       z=z-np.mean(z); z=np.tanh(2*z)
       p=np.exp(z); normalize
    return starts[:max_count]
```
Need p positive; `np.exp` finite.

def optimize_length(n, deadline, rng, height_seq):
    best_a = None; best_score = inf
    # base
    try:
       starts=...
       # if height: add resampled
       for s...
    except Exception as e: print
    if best_a is None: best_a=uniform; best_score=2
    print base score
    # support
    K_target=int(min(n, max(1, int(2.1*np.sqrt(n)))))
    support_S_best=None; support_seq_best=None; support_score=inf
    if time<deadline-120:
       support_candidates=[]
       if height_seq is not None:
          h=resample...
          support_candidates.append(np.argsort(h)[-K_target:].tolist())
       support_candidates.append(np.argsort(best_a)[-K_target:].tolist())
       support_candidates.append(np.sort(rng.choice(n,K_target,replace=False)).tolist()) # random
       support_budget = min(300.0, max(30.0, 0.25*(deadline-time)))
       per = support_budget / max(1,len(support_candidates))
       for si, S0 in enumerate(support_candidates):
          sub_deadline = time.min(deadline-60, time+per)
          S = support_local_search(n,S0,K_target,rng,sub_deadline)
          if S is not None and len(S)>0:
             seq=support_to_sequence(S,n); sc=fast_score(seq)
             update ...
    # support weight
    if support_S_best and time<deadline-80:
       weight_budget = min(240.0, max(30.0, 0.2*(deadline-time)))
       # run on best support, allocate
       seq=sc... = support_weight_lbfgs(support_S_best,n,time+weight_budget,p0=support_seq_best)
       update
       # if time, maybe run on second support? skip
    # full L-BFGS
    candidates=[best_a]
    if support_seq_best is not None: candidates.append(support_seq_best)
    # sort by current score
    keep top 2 unique
    remaining = deadline - time - 30
    if remaining > 10 and HAVE_SCIPY:
       fracs=(0.6,0.4)
       for i,cand in enumerate(candidates):
          alloc=remaining*fracs[i]
          sub_deadline=time+alloc
          seq, sc = log_lbfgs_refine(cand, sub_deadline, rng, n)
          update
          remaining = deadline - time - 30
    # exact polish
    if time < deadline-20:
       seq=sc=exact_gradient_polish(best_a, deadline-10, rng)
       update
    # final ensure
    best_a=normalize_simplex(best_a)
    return best_a, fast_score(best_a)
```

#### `exact_gradient_polish` implementation top-level
Similar previous but using beta factor based on M.
```
def exact_gradient_polish(a0, deadline, rng):
    a=normalize_simplex(a0)
    n=a.size; best_a=a.copy(); best_score=fast_score(a)
    step=1.0; no_improve=0; it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       it+=1
       b=fft_convolution(a); M=b.max()
       cur=2.0*n*M
       if cur < best_score - 1e-12:
          best_score=cur; best_a=a.copy(); no_improve=0
       else: no_improve +=1
       if no_improve > 1500: break
       beta = max(1.0, 5000.0 / max(M,1e-300))
       G,w=smooth_max_and_weights(b,beta)
       svec=corr_pw(a,w); g=2*svec; grad=g - g.mean()
       gnorm=np.linalg.norm(grad)
       if not finite or gnorm<1e-14: break
       accepted=False
       alphas=(1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001)
       for mult in alphas:
          alpha=step*mult
          q=project_simplex(a - alpha*grad,1.0)
          sc=fast_score(q)
          if sc < cur - 1e-13:
             a=q; accepted=True
             if sc < best_score: best...
             break
       if accepted:
          if step < 10.0: step=min(10.0, step*1.5)
          no_improve=0
       else:
          step *= 0.5
          if step < 1e-8:
             # perturb
             a = normalize_simplex(a * (1 + 1e-4*rng.normal(size=n)))
             step=0.1
          no_improve +=1
       if it%500==0: print
    return best_a,best_score
```

Let's implement `support_greedy_build` with optional time. It uses active bool for random inactive. It can maybe get stuck; try multiple random restarts inside? Just one restart with random candidate. We can do `restarts=2` maybe. But time allocate. Top-level function can take `deadline`.
```
def support_greedy_build(n,K_target,rng,deadline,max_col=3):
   best_S=[]; best_score=inf
   for restart in range(3):
      S=[]; active=np.zeros(n,bool); cnt=[0]*(2*n-1)
      for step in range(K_target):
         chosen=-1
         for _ in range(200):
            x=random_inactive(n,active,rng,5)
            if x==-1: break
            if cnt[2*x] > max_col-1: continue
            ok=True
            for y in S:
               if cnt[x+y] > max_col-2:
                  ok=False; break
            if ok: chosen=x; break
         if chosen==-1: break
         active[chosen]=True; S.append(chosen); cnt[2*chosen]+=1
         for y in S[:-1]: cnt[chosen+y]+=2
      if not S: continue
      maxc=max(cnt)
      sc=support_score(n,maxc,len(S))
      if sc<best_score: best_score=sc; best_S=S.copy()
      if len(S)==K_target and maxc<=max_col: break
      if time.monotonic()>=deadline: break
   return best_S
```
`S[:-1]` creates list each step; for performance use `for ii in range(len(S)-1): y=S[ii]`.

`random_inactive` uses tries maybe 5; if fail, return -1.

    z = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    z[ref] = 0.0
    if v.size:
        z[idx] = v
    zmax = float(np.max(z))

    z = z - zmax
    e = np.exp(np.clip(z, -745.0, 700.0))
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s<=0 or not finite:
       a=np.zeros(n); a[ref]=1; return a

```python
"""
Search for non-negative sequences minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.

Main ideas:
1. Exploit a sparse combinatorial viewpoint: if K active positions have
   pair-sum counts with maximum R and equal weights 1/K, the score is
   2*n*R/K^2.  We use constraint local search to build dense supports
   with small pair-sum maxima.
2. Optimize weights on a fixed support with L-BFGS in a softmax
   reparameterization.
3. Optimize the full sequence with L-BFGS in a softmax reparameterization.
4. Finish with a short projected-gradient / active-subgradient polish.
"""

import time

import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
    HAVE_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    HAVE_SCIPY = False


def normalize_simplex(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if p.size == 0:
        return p
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(p.shape, 1.0 / float(p.size), dtype=np.float64)
    p *= 1.0 / s
    return p


def fft_convolution(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([a[0] * a[0]], dtype=np.float64)

    need = 2 * n - 1
    size = 1
    while size < need:
        size <<= 1

    F = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    b = np.fft.irfft(F * F, size)[:need]
    b = np.ascontiguousarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    return np.maximum(b, 0.0)


def smooth_max_and_weights(b, beta):
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    if b.size == 0:
        return float("inf"), np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    m = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf"), np.zeros_like(b, dtype=np.float64)

    if m <= 0.0 or beta <= 0.0:
        w = np.ones_like(b, dtype=np.float64)
        w /= float(b.size)
        return m, w

    x = beta * (b - m)
    x = np.clip(x, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(x)
    Z = float(np.sum(e))

    if Z <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(Z):
        e = np.zeros_like(b, dtype=np.float64)
        e[int(np.argmax(b))] = 1.0
        Z = 1.0

    w = e / Z
    G = m + float(np.log(Z)) / beta
    return float(G), w


def corr_pw(p, w):
    """
    Return s_i = sum_j p_j w_{i+j}, used for d/dp_i sum_k w_k (p*p)_k.
    """
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    m = w.size
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([float(np.dot(p, w[:1]))], dtype=np.float64)

    need = n + m - 1
    size = 1
    while size < need:
        size <<= 1

    P = np.fft.rfft(p, size)
    WR = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], size)
    C = np.fft.irfft(P * WR, size)[:need]

    s = C[m - n : m][::-1].copy()
    return np.ascontiguousarray(s, dtype=np.float64)


def fast_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return float("inf")
    b = fft_convolution(a)
    m = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def resample_to_length(p, n):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    if p.size == 1:
        v = max(float(p[0]), 1e-300)
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)

    old = (np.arange(p.size, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(p.size)
    new = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    q = np.interp(new, old, p)
    return normalize_simplex(q)


def top_k_indices(a, k):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 0:
        return []
    if k >= n:
        return list(range(n))
    return np.argsort(a)[-k:].tolist()


def structured_starts(n, rng, max_count=8):
    starts = []
    starts.append(np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64))

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    for a in (-4.0, -2.0, 2.0, 4.0):
        p = np.exp(a * (x - 0.5))
        starts.append(normalize_simplex(p))

    for eps in (0.5, 0.8):
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        if n >= 2:
            half = n // 2
            p[:half] = 1.0 + eps
            p[half:] = max(1e-6, 1.0 - eps)
        starts.append(normalize_simplex(p))

    for k in (1, 2, 3):
        p = 1.0 + 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * x)
        starts.append(normalize_simplex(p))

    size = 1
    while size < n:
        size <<= 1

    for _ in range(2):
        zc = rng.normal(size=size) + 1j * rng.normal(size=size)
        z = np.real(np.fft.ifft(zc))[:n]
        z = z - float(np.mean(z))
        z = np.tanh(2.0 * z)
        p = np.exp(z)
        starts.append(normalize_simplex(p))

    return starts[:max_count]


def make_index_array(n, ref):
    idx = np.empty(n - 1, dtype=np.int64)
    if ref > 0:
        idx[:ref] = np.arange(ref, dtype=np.int64)
    if ref < n - 1:
        idx[ref:] = np.arange(ref + 1, n, dtype=np.int64)
    return idx


def indexed_softmax(v, ref, idx, n):
    z = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    z[ref] = 0.0
    if v.size:
        z[idx] = v

    zmax = float(np.max(z))
    if not np.isfinite(zmax):
        a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        a[ref] = 1.0
        return a

    z = z - zmax
    e = np.exp(np.clip(z, -745.0, 700.0))
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        a[ref] = 1.0
        return a

    e *= 1.0 / s
    return e


def log_lbfgs_objective(v, n, beta, ref, idx):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    if v.size:
        v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
        v = np.clip(v, -700.0, 700.0)

    a = indexed_softmax(v, ref, idx, n)
    b = fft_convolution(a)
    G, w = smooth_max_and_weights(b, beta)
    if not np.isfinite(G):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(max(0, n - 1), dtype=np.float64)

    svec = corr_pw(a, w)
    g = 2.0 * svec
    mean_g = float(np.dot(a, g))
    grad_z = a * (g - mean_g)

    val = float(n * G)
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(max(0, n - 1), dtype=np.float64)

    grad = grad_z[idx] * float(n)
    grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
    return val, grad


def log_lbfgs_refine(a0, deadline, rng, n):
    a = normalize_simplex(a0)
    if a.size != n:
        a = resample_to_length(a, n)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = fast_score(a)

    if n < 2:
        return best_a, best_score

    if n <= 4096:
        iters = (60, 90, 120, 180, 250)
        factors = (20.0, 80.0, 320.0, 1200.0, 5000.0)
    elif n <= 16384:
        iters = (40, 60, 90, 140, 200)
        factors = (20.0, 80.0, 320.0, 1200.0, 5000.0)
    else:
        iters = (30, 45, 70, 100, 150)
        factors = (20.0, 80.0, 320.0, 1200.0, 5000.0)

    for beta_factor, mi in zip(factors, iters):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 5.0:
            break

        b = fft_convolution(a)
        M = float(np.max(b))
        if M <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(M):
            break

        beta = max(1.0, beta_factor / M)

        ref = int(np.argmax(a))
        idx = make_index_array(n, ref)

        with np.errstate(divide="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
            loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
        v0 = loga[idx] - loga[ref]
        v0 = np.clip(np.where(np.isfinite(v0), v0, 0.0), -40.0, 40.0)

        bnds = [(-40.0, 40.0)] * (n - 1)
        options = {
            "maxiter": int(mi),
            "ftol": 1e-9,
            "gtol": 1e-6,
            "maxls": 20,
            "maxcor": 20,
        }

        try:
            res = minimize(
                log_lbfgs_objective,
                v0,
                args=(n, beta, ref, idx),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                bounds=bnds,
                options=options,
            )
            v = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
                continue
            a = indexed_softmax(v, ref, idx, n)
            a = normalize_simplex(a)
            sc = fast_score(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = a.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass
        finally:
            pass

    return best_a, best_score


def project_simplex(v, target=1.0):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    if v.size == 0:
        return v
    if target <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros_like(v, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
        return np.full(v.shape, target / float(v.size), dtype=np.float64)

    low = float(np.min(v)) - 1.0
    high = float(np.max(v)) + 1.0

    for _ in range(60):
        mid = 0.5 * (low + high)
        s = float(np.sum(np.maximum(v - mid, 0.0)))
        if s > target:
            low = mid
        else:
            high = mid

    tau = 0.5 * (low + high)
    q = np.maximum(v - tau, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(q))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        q = np.full(v.shape, target / float(v.size), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        q *= target / s
    return q


def exact_gradient_polish(a0, deadline, rng):
    a = normalize_simplex(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a, float("inf")

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = fast_score(a)
    step = 1.0
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        b = fft_convolution(a)
        M = float(np.max(b))
        cur = float(2.0 * n * M)

        if cur < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = cur
            best_a = a.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve > 1200:
            break

        beta = max(1.0, 5000.0 / max(M, 1e-300))
        G, w = smooth_max_and_weights(b, beta)
        if not np.isfinite(G):
            break

        svec = corr_pw(a, w)
        g = 2.0 * svec
        grad = g - float(np.mean(g))
        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if not np.isfinite(gnorm) or gnorm < 1e-14:
            break

        accepted = False
        for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02, 0.01, 0.005, 0.002, 0.001):
            alpha = step * mult
            q = project_simplex(a - alpha * grad, 1.0)
            sc = fast_score(q)
            if sc < cur - 1e-13:
                a = q
                accepted = True
                if sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = a.copy()
                break

        if accepted:
            no_improve = 0
            if step < 10.0:
                step = min(10.0, step * 1.35)
        else:
            step *= 0.5
            no_improve += 1
            if step < 1e-8:
                a = normalize_simplex(a * (1.0 + 1e-4 * rng.normal(size=n)))
                step = 0.1

        if it % 500 == 0:
            print(f"polish it={it} best={best_score:.8f} step={step:.3e}")

    return best_a, best_score


def support_score(n, maxc, K):
    if K <= 0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * maxc) / (float(K) * float(K))


def build_support_counts(S, n, bucket_size):
    length = 2 * n - 1
    cnt = [0] * length
    m = len(S)

    for ii in range(m):
        i = S[ii]
        cnt[i + i] += 1
    for ii in range(m):
        i = S[ii]
        for jj in range(ii + 1, m):
            cnt[i + S[jj]] += 2

    maxc = max(cnt) if length else 0
    size = max(bucket_size, maxc + 1)
    bucket = [0] * size

    for c in cnt:
        if c >= 0 and c < size:
            bucket[c] += 1

    return cnt, bucket, maxc


def delta_count(cnt, bucket, maxc, s, delta):
    if s < 0 or s >= len(cnt):
        return maxc

    old = cnt[s]
    new = old + delta
    if new < 0:
        new = 0

    if new >= len(bucket):
        bucket.extend([0] * (new + 1 - len(bucket)))

    bucket[old] -= 1
    cnt[s] = new
    bucket[new] += 1

    if new > maxc:
        maxc = new
    elif old == maxc and bucket[old] == 0:
        while maxc > 0 and bucket[maxc] == 0:
            maxc -= 1

    return maxc


def random_inactive(n, active, rng, tries=20):
    for _ in range(tries):
        x = int(rng.integers(n))
        if not active[x]:
            return x
    return -1


def support_try_add(S, active, cnt, bucket, maxc, n, rng, K_target):
    K = len(S)
    if K >= K_target:
        return maxc, False

    x = random_inactive(n, active, rng, 30)
    if x < 0:
        return maxc, False

    old_max = maxc
    old_score = support_score(n, maxc, K)

    maxc = delta_count(cnt, bucket, maxc, 2 * x, +1)
    for y in S:
        maxc = delta_count(cnt, bucket, maxc, x + y, +2)

    active[x] = True
    S.append(x)

    new_score = support_score(n, maxc, K + 1)

    if new_score < old_score - 1e-12:
        return maxc, True

    # revert
    for y in S:
        if y == x:
            maxc = delta_count(cnt, bucket, maxc, 2 * x, -1)
        else:
            maxc = delta_count(cnt, bucket, maxc, x + y, -2)

    if S and S[-1] == x:
        S.pop()
    active[x] = False
    return old_max, False


def support_try_swap(S, active, cnt, bucket, maxc, n, rng):
    K = len(S)
    if K < 2:
        return maxc, False

    pos_i = int(rng.integers(K))
    i = S[pos_i]
    j = random_inactive(n, active, rng, 30)
    if j < 0:
        return maxc, False

    old_max = maxc
    old_score = support_score(n, maxc, K)
    old_bucketmax = bucket[old_max] if old_max < len(bucket) else 0

    # remove i
    for y in S:
        if y == i:
            maxc = delta_count(cnt, bucket, maxc, 2 * i, -1)
        else:
            maxc = delta_count(cnt, bucket, maxc, i + y, -2)

    S[pos_i] = S[-1]
    S.pop()
    active[i] = False

    # add j
    for y in S:
        maxc = delta_count(cnt, bucket, maxc, j + y, +2)
    maxc = delta_count(cnt, bucket, maxc, 2 * j, +1)

    S.append(j)
    active[j] = True

    new_score = support_score(n, maxc, K)
    new_bucketmax = bucket[maxc] if maxc < len(bucket) else 0

    accept = new_score < old_score - 1e-12
    if (not accept) and maxc == old_max and new_bucketmax < old_bucketmax:
        accept = True

    if accept:
        return maxc, True

    # revert: remove j, add i
    for y in S:
        if y == j:
            maxc = delta_count(cnt, bucket, maxc, 2 * j, -1)
        else:
            maxc = delta_count(cnt, bucket, maxc, j + y, -2)

    if S and S[-1] == j:
        S.pop()
    active[j] = False

    for y in S:
        maxc = delta_count(cnt, bucket, maxc, i + y, +2)
    maxc = delta_count(cnt, bucket, maxc, 2 * i, +1)

    S.append(i)
    active[i] = True

    return old_max, False


def support_local_search(n, S0, K_target, rng, deadline):
    S = list(set(int(x) for x in S0 if 0 <= x < n))
    if len(S) > K_target:
        S = S[:K_target]

    active = np.zeros(n, dtype=bool)
    for x in S:
        active[x] = True

    # Fill to target with random inactive positions. Large-K starts are often
    # useful because the score denominator grows with K^2.
    tries_fill = 0
    while len(S) < K_target and tries_fill < 10000 and time.monotonic() < deadline:
        tries_fill += 1
        x = random_inactive(n, active, rng, 5)
        if x >= 0:
            S.append(x)
            active[x] = True

    if len(S) == 0:
        return []

    bucket_size = max(16, K_target + 16)
    cnt, bucket, maxc = build_support_counts(S, n, bucket_size)
    cur_score = support_score(n, maxc, len(S))

    best_S = S.copy()
    best_score = cur_score

    no_accept = 0
    trials = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline and trials < 2000000:
        trials += 1
        K = len(S)

        if K < K_target and (rng.random() < 0.45):
            cur_max = maxc
            _, accepted = support_try_add(S, active, cnt, bucket, cur_max, n, rng, K_target)
            maxc = cur_max
            cur_score = support_score(n, maxc, len(S))
        else:
            cur_max = maxc
            maxc, accepted = support_try_swap(S, active, cnt, bucket, cur_max, n, rng)
            cur_score = support_score(n, maxc, len(S))

        if accepted:
            no_accept = 0
            if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score = cur_score
                best_S = S.copy()
        else:
            no_accept += 1
            if no_accept > 12000:
                # Restart by perturbing the current support.
                frac = max(1, len(S) // 8)
                S_set = set(S)
                cand = list(S)
                for _ in range(frac * 3):
                    if len(cand) <= 1:
                        break
                    pos = int(rng.integers(len(cand)))
                    x = int(rng.integers(n))
                    if x in S_set:
                        continue
                    old = cand[pos]
                    cand[pos] = x
                    S_set.discard(old)
                    S_set.add(x)
                S = cand[:K_target]
                active = np.zeros(n, dtype=bool)
                for x in S:
                    active[x] = True
                cnt, bucket, maxc = build_support_counts(S, n, bucket_size)
                cur_score = support_score(n, maxc, len(S))
                no_accept = 0
                if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best_score = cur_score
                    best_S = S.copy()

        if trials % 5000 == 0:
            cnt, bucket, maxc = build_support_counts(S, n, bucket_size)
            cur_score = support_score(n, maxc, len(S))
            if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score = cur_score
                best_S = S.copy()
            if trials % 20000 == 0:
                print(
                    f"support local n={n} K={len(S)} max={maxc} "
                    f"score={cur_score:.8f} best={best_score:.8f}"
                )

    return best_S


def support_greedy_build(n, K_target, rng, deadline, max_col=3):
    best_S = []
    best_score = float("inf")

    for restart in range(2):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        S = []
        active = np.zeros(n, dtype=bool)
        cnt = [0] * (2 * n - 1)

        for step in range(K_target):
            chosen = -1
            for _ in range(160):
                x = random_inactive(n, active, rng, 5)
                if x < 0:
                    break
                if cnt[2 * x] > max_col - 1:
                    continue
                ok = True
                for y in S:
                    if cnt[x + y] > max_col - 2:
                        ok = False
                        break
                if ok:
                    chosen = x
                    break

            if chosen < 0:
                break

            old_len = len(S)
            active[chosen] = True
            S.append(chosen)
            cnt[2 * chosen] += 1
            for ii in range(old_len):
                cnt[chosen + S[ii]] += 2

        if not S:
            continue

        maxc = max(cnt)
        sc = support_score(n, maxc, len(S))
        if sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_S = S.copy()

        if len(S) == K_target and maxc <= max_col:
            break

    return best_S


def support_to_sequence(S, n, floor=1e-12):
    S = list(set(int(x) for x in S if 0 <= x < n))
    if not S:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.full(n, floor, dtype=np.float64)
    for x in S:
        a[x] = 1.0
    return normalize_simplex(a)


def support_weight_objective(v, S, n, beta, ref, idx):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    if v.size:
        v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
        v = np.clip(v, -700.0, 700.0)

    p = indexed_softmax(v, ref, idx, len(S))
    full = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    full[S] = p

    b = fft_convolution(full)
    G, w = smooth_max_and_weights(b, beta)
    if not np.isfinite(G):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(max(0, len(S) - 1), dtype=np.float64)

    svec = corr_pw(full, w)
    g = svec[S] * 2.0
    mean_g = float(np.dot(p, g))
    grad_z = p * (g - mean_g)

    val = float(n * G)
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(max(0, len(S) - 1), dtype=np.float64)

    grad = grad_z[idx] * float(n)
    grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
    return val, grad


def support_weight_lbfgs(S, n, deadline, p0=None):
    S = np.asarray(list(set(int(x) for x in S if 0 <= x < n)), dtype=np.int64)
    K = S.size
    if K == 0:
        seq = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
        return seq, fast_score(seq)
    if K == 1:
        seq = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        seq[S[0]] = 1.0
        return seq, fast_score(seq)

    if p0 is None:
        p = np.full(K, 1.0 / float(K), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        p0 = np.asarray(p0, dtype=np.float64)
        if p0.size != n:
            p0 = resample_to_length(p0, n)
        p = p0[S]
        p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
        s = float(np.sum(p))
        if s <= 1e-300:
            p = np.full(K, 1.0 / float(K), dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            p = p / s

    def sequence_from_p(pv):
        full = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        full[S] = pv
        return normalize_simplex(full)

    seq_best = sequence_from_p(p)
    score_best = fast_score(seq_best)

    if K < 2:
        return seq_best, score_best

    iters = (120, 180, 240, 320, 420)
    factors = (20.0, 80.0, 320.0, 1200.0, 5000.0)

    for beta_factor, mi in zip(factors, iters):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 5.0:
            break

        b = fft_convolution(seq_best)
        M = float(np.max(b))
        if M <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(M):
            break

        beta = max(1.0, beta_factor / M)
        ref = int(np.argmax(p))
        idx = make_index_array(K, ref)

        with np.errstate(divide="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
            logp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
        v0 = logp[idx] - logp[ref]
        v0 = np.clip(np.where(np.isfinite(v0), v0, 0.0), -40.0, 40.0)

        bnds = [(-40.0, 40.0)] * (K - 1)
        options = {
            "maxiter": int(mi),
            "ftol": 1e-9,
            "gtol": 1e-6,
            "maxls": 20,
            "maxcor": 20,
        }

        try:
            res = minimize(
                support_weight_objective,
                v0,
                args=(S, n, beta, ref, idx),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                bounds=bnds,
                options=options,
            )
            v = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
                continue
            p = indexed_softmax(v, ref, idx, K)
            p = normalize_simplex(p)
            seq = sequence_from_p(p)
            sc = fast_score(seq)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < score_best:
                score_best = sc
                seq_best = seq
        except Exception:
            pass

    return seq_best, score_best


def optimize_length(n, deadline, rng, height_seq):
    best_a = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    # Base continuous starts.
    try:
        starts = []
        height_n = None
        if height_seq is not None:
            height_n = resample_to_length(height_seq, n)
            starts.append(height_n)
        starts.extend(structured_starts(n, rng, max_count=8))

        for s in starts:
            sc = fast_score(s)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = s.copy()
    except Exception:
        pass

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
        best_score = fast_score(best_a)

    print(f"base best n={n} score={best_score:.8f}")

    # Common dense-support target. Large K helps even if pair-sum max is 3 or 4.
    K_target = int(min(n, max(1, int(2.35 * np.sqrt(float(n))))))

    support_S_best = None
    support_seq_best = None
    support_score_best = float("inf")

    # Support search.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 120.0:
        support_candidates = []
        greedy_deadline = max(time.monotonic() + 5.0, min(deadline - 60.0, time.monotonic() + 30.0))
        try:
            greedy_S = support_greedy_build(n, K_target, rng, greedy_deadline, max_col=3)
            if len(greedy_S) > 0:
                support_candidates.append(greedy_S)
        except Exception:
            pass

        if height_n is not None:
            support_candidates.append(top_k_indices(height_n, K_target))
        support_candidates.append(top_k_indices(best_a, K_target))
        support_candidates.append(list(rng.choice(n, size=K_target, replace=False)))

        support_budget = min(420.0, max(30.0, 0.30 * (deadline - time.monotonic())))
        per = support_budget / float(max(1, len(support_candidates)))

        for idx, S0 in enumerate(support_candidates):
            sub_deadline = time.monotonic() + per
            sub_deadline = min(sub_deadline, deadline - 60.0)
            if sub_deadline <= time.monotonic() + 10.0:
                break
            try:
                S = support_local_search(n, S0, K_target, rng, sub_deadline)
                if len(S) > 0:
                    seq = support_to_sequence(S, n)
                    sc = fast_score(seq)
                    if np.isfinite(sc):
                        if sc < support_score_best:
                            support_score_best = sc
                            support_S_best = S
                            support_seq_best = seq
                        print(f"support cand {idx} score={sc:.8f}")
                        if sc < best_score:
                            best_score = sc
                            best_a = seq.copy()
            except Exception:
                pass

    # Weight optimization on the best fixed support.
    if support_S_best is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 90.0:
        weight_budget = min(300.0, max(30.0, 0.22 * (deadline - time.monotonic())))
        sub_deadline = min(deadline - 30.0, time.monotonic() + weight_budget)
        try:
            seq, sc = support_weight_lbfgs(support_S_best, n, sub_deadline, p0=support_seq_best)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < support_score_best:
                support_score_best = sc
                support_seq_best = seq
            if support_seq_best is not None and support_score_best < best_score:
                best_score = support_score_best
                best_a = support_seq_best.copy()
                print(f"support weights score={best_score:.8f}")
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Full-sequence L-BFGS from the best continuous and support sequences.
    candidates = []
    if best_a is not None:
        candidates.append(best_a)
    if support_seq_best is not None:
        candidates.append(support_seq_best)

    # Sort candidates by their current score and keep at most two distinct ones.
    if HAVE_SCIPY and len(candidates) > 0:
        scored = []
        for c in candidates:
            sc = fast_score(c)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                scored.append((sc, c))
        scored.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])
        top = []
        for sc, c in scored:
            if len(top) >= 2:
                break
            dup = False
            for d in top:
                if d.size == c.size and float(np.max(np.abs(d - c))) < 1e-8:
                    dup = True
                    break
            if not dup:
                top.append(c)

        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic() - 30.0
        if remaining > 60.0:
            fracs = (0.60, 0.40) if len(top) >= 2 else (1.0,)
            for i, c in enumerate(top):
                alloc = remaining * fracs[i]
                sub_deadline = time.monotonic() + alloc
                sub_deadline = min(sub_deadline, deadline - 30.0)
                if sub_deadline <= time.monotonic() + 15.0:
                    break
                try:
                    seq, sc = log_lbfgs_refine(c, sub_deadline, rng, n)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                        best_score = sc
                        best_a = seq.copy()
                        print(f"full lbfgs start {i} score={best_score:.8f}")
                except Exception:
                    pass

                remaining = deadline - time.monotonic() - 30.0
                if remaining < 60.0:
                    break

    # Final short exact polish.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 25.0:
        polish_deadline = min(deadline - 5.0, time.monotonic() + min(90.0, 0.08 * (deadline - time.monotonic())))
        try:
            seq, sc = exact_gradient_polish(best_a, polish_deadline, rng)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = seq.copy()
                print(f"polish final score={best_score:.8f}")
        except Exception:
            pass

    best_a = normalize_simplex(best_a)
    best_score = fast_score(best_a)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_a = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
        best_score = 2.0

    return best_a, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0):
    try:
        seed = int(seed)
    except Exception:
        seed = 42

    try:
        budget_s = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget_s = 2400.0

    if budget_s <= 0.0:
        return [1.0]

    start_time = time.monotonic()

    if budget_s < 300.0:
        N = 2048
        reserve = 45.0
    elif budget_s < 800.0:
        N = 4096
        reserve = 75.0
    elif budget_s < 1600.0:
        N = 8192
        reserve = 105.0
    else:
        N = 16384
        reserve = 150.0

    deadline = start_time + max(0.0, budget_s - reserve)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    height = None
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if h is not None:
            h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64)
            if h.size > 0:
                height = h
    except Exception:
        height = None

    try:
        seq, score = optimize_length(N, deadline, rng, height)
        print(f"final fast score N={N} score={score:.8f}")
    except Exception:
        print("optimize_length failed; using fallback")
        seq = None

    if seq is None or seq.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(seq)):
        seq = np.full(N, 1.0 / float(N), dtype=np.float64)

    seq = normalize_simplex(seq)
    seq = np.maximum(seq, 0.0)
    seq = np.minimum(seq, 1000.0)

    return [float(x) for x in seq]
```
